`演出后，语法班坐在地面向舞台下倾的位置，一名疲倦学生垂下头，随后学习列出一个可变格拉丁名词的词形变化。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:decline14 -->
A supposed high yielder's quoted yield did not guarantee what the investment would yield, especially among the screen's decliners, and a decline in income could matter more than an attractive percentage as its value declined.
`所谓高收益投资标出的收益率，不能保证它实际带来多少回报，尤其当它已列在屏幕的下跌品种中时；其价值下跌时，收入减少可能比一个漂亮百分比更重要。`

<!-- WORD:W014 -->
# discriminate

<!-- EXAMPLE:discriminate01 -->
He is incapable of discriminating between a good idea and a terrible one.
`他无法分辨好主意与糟糕主意。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:discriminate01 -->
cannot discriminate between right and wrong
`无法辨别是非。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:discriminate02 -->
They believe the law discriminates against women.
`他们认为这部法律歧视女性。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:discriminate02 -->
…legislation which would discriminate in favour of racial minorities.
`……会给予少数族裔优待的立法。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:discriminate03 -->
The cook could discriminate among subtle flavours, and discriminating visitors chose their samples discriminatingly, exercising discrimination in taste without using taste as a reason to rank people.
`厨师能鉴别细微风味，有眼力的访客也仔细挑选试吃品，运用味觉鉴赏力，却不拿品味当作给人分高下的理由。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:discriminate04 -->
many things discriminate one person from another
`许多特征使一个人与另一个人区分开来。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:discriminate05 -->
Careful discrimination between the two rules let the committee act discriminately, but its electronic ticket checker needed a discriminative test and a working discriminator to distinguish valid codes from damaged ones.
`仔细区分两项规则，让委员会能够有区别地行动，而电子验票器也需要具有辨别作用的测试，以及能区分有效代码和损坏代码的鉴别装置。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:discriminate06 -->
Careful discrimination between the two rules let the committee act discriminately, but its electronic ticket checker needed a discriminative test and a working discriminator to distinguish valid codes from damaged ones.
`仔细区分两项规则，让委员会能够有区别地行动，而电子验票器也需要具有辨别作用的测试，以及能区分有效代码和损坏代码的鉴别装置。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:discriminate07 -->
Careful discrimination between the two rules let the committee act discriminately, but its electronic ticket checker needed a discriminative test and a working discriminator to distinguish valid codes from damaged ones.
`仔细区分两项规则，让委员会能够有区别地行动，而电子验票器也需要具有辨别作用的测试，以及能区分有效代码和损坏代码的鉴别装置。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:discriminate08 -->
Mira could discriminate between an access requirement and an irrelevant preference, and she refused to discriminate against an applicant merely to preserve a discriminatory custom.
`米拉能分清准入要求与无关偏好，也拒绝仅为保留一项歧视性惯例而歧视申请者。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:discriminate09 -->
More discriminating visitors now tend to shun the area.
`现在更有鉴赏力的游客往往会避开这个地区。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:discriminate10 -->
The cook's precise seasoning discriminated her soup from the others, and that discriminating feature belonged to a dish, whereas discrimination against a volunteer belonged to an unfair practice that the centre would challenge.
`厨师精确的调味使她的汤区别于其他汤品，这种区别性特征属于菜肴；相反，对志愿者的歧视属于中心必须质疑的不公平做法。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:discriminate11 -->
The cook could discriminate among subtle flavours, and discriminating visitors chose their samples discriminatingly, exercising discrimination in taste without using taste as a reason to rank people.
`厨师能鉴别细微风味，有眼力的访客也仔细挑选试吃品，运用味觉鉴赏力，却不拿品味当作给人分高下的理由。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:discriminate12 -->
She is exempt from sex discrimination laws.
`她不在性别歧视法律的适用范围内。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:discriminate12 -->
…discrimination against immigrants.
`……对移民的歧视。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:discriminate13 -->
They cooked without skill and ate without discrimination.
`他们做菜没有技巧，吃东西也不讲究。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:discriminate14 -->
…colour discrimination.
`……辨色能力。`

<!-- WORD:W015 -->
# disrupt

<!-- EXAMPLE:disrupt01 -->
Anti-war protesters disrupted the debate.
`反战抗议者打断了辩论。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:disrupt02 -->
If it succeeds, the technology has the potential to seriously disrupt the current market for medical devices.
`如果成功，这项技术可能会大幅改变当前医疗器械市场的运行方式。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:disrupt03 -->
That infrastructural question mattered more than the launch poster, especially after a burst pipe disrupted a plaster partition and forced them to protect the wiring behind it.
`这一基础设施问题比发布海报更重要，尤其是一根水管爆裂、冲裂石膏隔墙，迫使他们保护墙后的电线之后。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:disrupt04 -->
A visiting designer called her booking tool a disruptor, but Mira asked how it would disrupt the old market and whether its disruptive method would actually improve the visitor's choices.
`一位来访设计师把自己的预约工具称为改变行业者，但米拉追问，它将如何改变原有市场，以及这种变革方法是否真能改善访客的选择。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:disrupt05 -->
The strike is expected to cause delays and disruption to flights from Britain.
`预计罢工会使从英国出发的航班延误并扰乱航班运行。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:disrupt06 -->
There are many ways children's disruptive behaviour can be managed.
`有许多办法可以处理孩子扰乱秩序的行为。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:disrupt07 -->
One example of disruptive technology is 3-D printing.
`三维打印就是一项改变既有模式的技术实例。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:disrupt08 -->
A staffed desk could be a mitigator of confusion, and its mitigatory role mattered more than a display that flashed disruptively and added to the disruptiveness of an already disruptive queue.
`有人值守的接待台能够减少混乱，这种缓解作用比一个扰乱人心地闪烁、又加重本已混乱队伍之干扰程度的显示屏更有价值。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:disrupt09 -->
A staffed desk could be a mitigator of confusion, and its mitigatory role mattered more than a display that flashed disruptively and added to the disruptiveness of an already disruptive queue.
`有人值守的接待台能够减少混乱，这种缓解作用比一个扰乱人心地闪烁、又加重本已混乱队伍之干扰程度的显示屏更有价值。`

<!-- WORD:W016 -->
# domestic

<!-- EXAMPLE:domestic01 -->
…over 100 domestic flights a day to 15 U.K. destinations.
`……每天一百多个飞往英国十五个目的地的国内航班。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:domestic01 -->
…sales in the domestic market.
`……国内市场的销售。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:domestic02 -->
…a plan for sharing domestic chores.
`……一份分担家务的计划。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:domestic03 -->
…domestic appliances.
`……家用电器。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:domestic04 -->
It was a scene of such domestic bliss.
`那是多么幸福温馨的家庭景象。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:domestic05 -->
…a domestic cat.
`……一只家猫。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:domestic06 -->
In the kitchen, a former domestic who loved domesticity was thoroughly domestic in her tastes, yet she insisted that domestic chores and the domestic appliance demonstration should be shared work, not a portrait of domestic bliss purchased by someone else's unpaid labour.
`厨房里，一位曾任家务工、喜爱家庭生活的人很爱家，却坚持家务和家用电器演示都应共同分担，而不应让别人无偿劳动来换取一幅家庭幸福图景。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:domestic07 -->
Yle's account discussed food inflation and costs including wages, taxes and transport, while Finnish preferences for domestic products helped explain why domestic production mattered to the comparison.
`芬兰广播公司的报道讨论了食品通胀以及工资、税收、运输等成本，而芬兰消费者对本国产品的偏好，也有助于解释为什么本地生产会影响比较。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:domestic08 -->
In the kitchen, a former domestic who loved domesticity was thoroughly domestic in her tastes, yet she insisted that domestic chores and the domestic appliance demonstration should be shared work, not a portrait of domestic bliss purchased by someone else's unpaid labour.
`厨房里，一位曾任家务工、喜爱家庭生活的人很爱家，却坚持家务和家用电器演示都应共同分担，而不应让别人无偿劳动来换取一幅家庭幸福图景。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:domestic09 -->
Opportunities will improve as the company expands domestically and internationally.
`随着公司在国内和国际扩张，机会将会增多。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:domestic10 -->
We domesticated the dog to help us with hunting.
`我们驯化狗以帮助狩猎。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:domestic11 -->
The small appliance business sold domestically, and its owner joked that learning to cook had domesticated him more effectively than any lecture on home management.
`那家小电器商家在国内销售，老板开玩笑说，学做饭比任何家政讲座都更有效地使他习惯了家庭生活。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:domestic12 -->
A nature teacher put a photograph of a monitor beside one of a domestic dog, asking why a domesticable animal and a familiar pet were not the same category and how people domesticate species over time through domestication.
`一位自然课教师把巨蜥照片放在家犬照片旁，问大家：可以驯化的动物为何不等于熟悉的宠物，人类又怎样通过长期驯化使物种适应人类生活。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:domestic13 -->
A nature teacher put a photograph of a monitor beside one of a domestic dog, asking why a domesticable animal and a familiar pet were not the same category and how people domesticate species over time through domestication.
`一位自然课教师把巨蜥照片放在家犬照片旁，问大家：可以驯化的动物为何不等于熟悉的宠物，人类又怎样通过长期驯化使物种适应人类生活。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:domestic14 -->
…a small rebellion against routine and cosy domesticity.
`……对规律而安适的家庭生活的一次小小反抗。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:domestic15 -->
Leena also rejected a leap from one basket to gross domestic product, since understanding national output would require far more information about production, services and infrastructure than their small exercise supplied.
`莉娜也否决了从一个食品篮子跳到国内生产总值的推论，因为理解全国产出，需要关于生产、服务和基础设施的更多信息，而他们的小练习并未提供这些信息。`

<!-- WORD:W017 -->
# eligible

<!-- EXAMPLE:eligible01 -->
Almost half the population are eligible to vote in today's election.
`近半人口有资格在今天的选举中投票。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:eligible01 -->
You could be eligible for a university scholarship.
`你可能有资格获得大学奖学金。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:eligible01 -->
eligible for a rebate
`符合取得返款的资格。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:eligible02 -->
He's the most eligible bachelor in Japan.
`他是日本最受青睐的单身汉。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:eligible03 -->
The rules covering eligibility for benefits changed in the 1980s.
`有关福利领取资格的规则在二十世纪八十年代发生了变化。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:eligible04 -->
Leena placed each properly documented applicant eligibly on the list, and entered every exempt in a separate column showing the reason for the exemption.
`莉娜将资料齐全的申请者按合格条件列入名单，并把每位获豁免者记在另一栏，写明豁免原因。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:eligible05 -->
They were ineligible to remain in the U.S.A.
`他们不具备继续留在美国的资格。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:eligible06 -->
At a fictional matchmaking scene in the pupils' play, an eligible bachelor rejected the idea that a neighbour was an ineligible partner merely because she preferred a different way of living.
`在学生剧中的虚构相亲场景里，一位被视为理想婚配对象的单身男子，拒绝仅因邻居选择不同生活方式就把她视为不合适的伴侣。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:eligible07 -->
Anyone who had missed the briefing was initially ineligible, but an authorised exemption could exempt a person who completed an equivalent session, leaving them exempt from repeating it rather than registered ineligibly despite known ineligibility.
`错过说明会的人最初不合资格，但经过授权的豁免可以免除已完成同等课程者重复参加的要求；这样一来，他们是合法免去重修，而不是明知不具资格仍违规登记。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:eligible08 -->
Anyone who had missed the briefing was initially ineligible, but an authorised exemption could exempt a person who completed an equivalent session, leaving them exempt from repeating it rather than registered ineligibly despite known ineligibility.
`错过说明会的人最初不合资格，但经过授权的豁免可以免除已完成同等课程者重复参加的要求；这样一来，他们是合法免去重修，而不是明知不具资格仍违规登记。`

<!-- WORD:W018 -->
# evidence

<!-- EXAMPLE:evidence01 -->
Ganley said he'd seen no evidence of widespread fraud.
`甘利说他没有看到大范围欺诈的证据。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:evidence01 -->
There is a lot of evidence that stress is partly responsible for disease.
`有许多证据表明压力是致病的部分原因。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:evidence02 -->
The evidence against him was purely circumstantial.
`对他不利的证据全是间接证据。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:evidence02 -->
…enough evidence for a successful prosecution.
`……足以成功起诉的证据。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:evidence03 -->
The forensic scientists who carried out the original tests will be called to give evidence.
`实施最初检验的法证科学家将被传唤作证。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:evidence04 -->
He's wise in other ways too, as evidenced by his reason for switching from tennis to golf.
`从他把网球改成高尔夫球的理由可以看出，他在其他方面也很明智。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:evidence04 -->
She was not calculating and evidenced no specific interest in money.
`她并不工于算计，也没有表现出对金钱的特别兴趣。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:evidence05 -->
Few soldiers were in evidence.
`很少看到士兵。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:evidence06 -->
The old philosopher in their script valued the axiom's evidence, its immediate clearness to the mind, more than the number of witnesses agreeing with it.
`他们剧本里的老哲学家更看重公理的显然性，即心智立即看清它的那种明白程度，而不是有多少证人同意它。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:evidence07 -->
At a rehearsal for the afternoon's mock hearing, a clerk would call a witness in evidence to give evidence, ask her to verify her written statement on oath, and let the judge sustain an objection if the evidence failed to sustain a charge, before hearing anything offered in mitigation.
`在为下午模拟听证而作的排练中，书记员会传唤证人作证，请她以宣誓方式证实书面陈述；若证据无法支持指控，法官便可认可异议，然后再听取任何减轻情节的说明。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:evidence08 -->
His footprints were clearly evident in the heavy dust.
`厚厚尘土中的脚印清晰可见。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:evidence08 -->
…the best-publicised cases of evident injustice.
`……那些被最广泛报道、明显不公的案例。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:evidence08 -->
It was evident that she had once been a beauty.
`显然，她年轻时曾是美人。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:evidence09 -->
The man wore a bathrobe and had evidently just come from the bathroom.
`那男人穿着浴袍，显然刚从浴室出来。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:evidence09 -->
The two Russians evidently knew each other.
`这两个俄罗斯人显然互相认识。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:evidence09 -->
Quite evidently, it has nothing to do with social background.
`十分明显，这与社会背景无关。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:evidence09 -->
evidently, we're too late
`看来我们来得太晚了。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:evidence10 -->
From childhood, he was evidently at once rebellious and precocious.
`看来他从小就既叛逆又早慧。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:evidence11 -->
Leena replied, “Evidently.”
`莉娜回答：“看来是这样。”`

<!-- EXAMPLE:evidence12 -->
The receipts had evidential value because they recorded actual purchases, but evidentially they said nothing about unlisted goods, and that evidentiary gap stayed in the note.
`收据具有证据价值，因为它们记录了实际购买，但从证据角度说，它们无法说明未列商品的情况，因此这项证据缺口保留在说明中。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:evidence13 -->
The receipts had evidential value because they recorded actual purchases, but evidentially they said nothing about unlisted goods, and that evidentiary gap stayed in the note.
`收据具有证据价值，因为它们记录了实际购买，但从证据角度说，它们无法说明未列商品的情况，因此这项证据缺口保留在说明中。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:evidence14 -->
The receipts had evidential value because they recorded actual purchases, but evidentially they said nothing about unlisted goods, and that evidentiary gap stayed in the note.
`收据具有证据价值，因为它们记录了实际购买，但从证据角度说，它们无法说明未列商品的情况，因此这项证据缺口保留在说明中。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:evidence15 -->
It is self-evident that we will never have enough resources to meet the demand.
`显而易见，我们永远不可能有足够资源满足需求。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:evidence16 -->
Their limited coverage was self-evident once the list was visible, yet the self-evidence of that limitation did not make every proposed explanation self-evidently true.
`清单一摆出来，覆盖范围有限便不言而喻，但这一限制的显而易见，并不意味着每一种解释都自然正确。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:evidence17 -->
The task was self-evidently impossible.
`这项任务显然不可能完成。`

<!-- WORD:W019 -->
# exempt

<!-- EXAMPLE:exempt01 -->
Men in college were exempt from military service.
`在大学就读的男子免服兵役。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:exempt02 -->
South Carolina claimed the power to exempt its citizens from the obligation to obey federal law.
`南卡罗来纳州声称有权豁免本州公民遵守联邦法律的义务。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:exempt03 -->
Leena placed each properly documented applicant eligibly on the list, and entered every exempt in a separate column showing the reason for the exemption.
`莉娜将资料齐全的申请者按合格条件列入名单，并把每位获豁免者记在另一栏，写明豁免原因。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:exempt04 -->
An exempt commanded the royal guard, a noble's retainer carried a message, and the actor playing an old retainer teased his lifelong friend while the guard performed secure arms with wooden rifles to keep their locks from the imaginary rain.
`一位王室仪仗队指挥官指挥卫队，一名贵族家臣传递消息，而扮演忠实老友的演员打趣着相交一生的朋友；卫兵用木制步枪表演倒挟枪姿势，保护枪机免遭想象中的雨水。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:exempt05 -->
…the exemption of employer-provided health insurance from taxation.
`……雇主提供的医疗保险免税这一安排。`

<!-- WORD:W020 -->
# expenditure

<!-- EXAMPLE:expenditure01 -->
Policies of tax reduction must lead to reduced public expenditure.
`减税政策必然导致公共支出减少。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:expenditure01 -->
They should cut their expenditure on defence.
`他们应该削减国防开支。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:expenditure02 -->
The financial rewards justified the expenditure of effort.
`这些经济回报使所付努力显得值得。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:expenditure03 -->
Children expend a lot of energy and may need more high-energy food than adults.
`儿童消耗大量能量，可能比成年人更需要高能量食物。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:expenditure04 -->
Once our services cease to be useful to them, we're expendable.
`一旦我们的服务对他们不再有用，我们就成了可被抛弃的人。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:expenditure04 -->
During the recession, training budgets were seen as an expendable luxury.
`经济衰退期间，培训预算被视为可以舍弃的奢侈开支。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:expenditure05 -->
Mira maintained the cause of clear public information by rejecting a councillor's suggestion that the conditions were expendable details too unimportant to print.
`米拉支持清楚传播公共信息的事业，因而拒绝了一位委员的提议：他竟把条件说明视为无足轻重、可以不印的细节。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:expenditure06 -->
The committee could call a luxury expendable or use an expendable paper filter once, but Mira rejected the supposed expendability of helpers and refused to deploy them expendably as if their time were unimportant.
`委员会可以把奢侈品视为可舍弃物，也可以只用一次一次性纸滤片，但米拉拒绝所谓“帮手可以牺牲”的想法，不愿把他们当成时间无足轻重、可任意消耗的人来使用。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:expenditure07 -->

<!-- WORD:W021 -->
# fluctuate

<!-- EXAMPLE:fluctuate01 -->
Body temperature can fluctuate if you are ill.
`生病时体温可能会波动。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:fluctuate01 -->
…the fluctuating price of oil.
`……不断波动的石油价格。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:fluctuate02 -->
Don't worry about tiny fluctuations in your weight.
`不要为体重的微小波动担心。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:fluctuate02 -->
The calculations do not take into account any fluctuation in the share price.
`这些计算没有考虑股价的任何波动。`

<!-- WORD:W022 -->
# incentive

<!-- EXAMPLE:incentive01 -->
There is little or no incentive to adopt such measures.
`几乎没有或完全没有采取这类措施的动力。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:incentive02 -->
A budget shop's cheap coffee might be an incentive to enter, while an incentive payment for extra sales would give its staff another incentive, so the family examined both prices and motives.
`低价商店的便宜咖啡可能吸引顾客进店，而额外销售的奖励金会给员工另一种动力，因此这个家庭同时考察价格与动机。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:incentive03 -->
A budget shop's cheap coffee might be an incentive to enter, while an incentive payment for extra sales would give its staff another incentive, so the family examined both prices and motives.
`低价商店的便宜咖啡可能吸引顾客进店，而额外销售的奖励金会给员工另一种动力，因此这个家庭同时考察价格与动机。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:incentive04 -->
High marginal tax rates may act as a disincentive to working longer hours.
`较高边际税率可能使人不愿延长工作时间。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:incentive05 -->
For them, a long journey was a disincentive to buying a small bargain, and a disincentive fee for changing a delivery time made a seemingly cheap order less attractive.
`对他们而言，漫长路程会削弱购买小额优惠商品的意愿，而用于抑制改约的送货时间变更费，也会让看似便宜的订单失去吸引力。`

<!-- WORD:W023 -->
# inflation

<!-- EXAMPLE:inflation01 -->
…rising unemployment and high inflation.
`……失业率上升而通胀高企。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:inflation01 -->
…an inflation rate of only 2.2%.
`……仅为百分之二点二的通胀率。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:inflation02 -->
In the economics discussion, one inflationist defended inflationism and an inflationist policy that would inflate the currency, while another participant asked how far that monetary inflation would create inflationary pressure rather than treating every cause of rising prices as identical.
`经济讨论中，一位通胀政策支持者主张货币扩张思想及相应政策，另一位参与者则询问，这种货币膨胀在多大程度上会造成通胀压力，而没有把所有涨价原因都当成同一件事。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:inflation03 -->
A volunteer used an inflater to inflate a demonstration bag, stopped its inflation at the marked limit, and let a flexible valve yield under gentle pressure before putting the inflator away.
`一名志愿者用充气装置给演示袋充气，到标线便停止充气，再让柔性阀在轻压下让开，随后收起充气器。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:inflation04 -->
The bank is worried about mounting inflationary pressures.
`银行担心不断加剧的通胀压力。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:inflation05 -->

<!-- EXAMPLE:inflation06 -->

`经济讨论中，一位通胀政策支持者主张货币扩张思想及相应政策，另一位参与者则询问，这种货币膨胀在多大程度上会造成通胀压力，而没有把所有涨价原因都当成同一件事。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:inflation07 -->
In the economics discussion, one inflationist defended inflationism and an inflationist policy that would inflate the currency, while another participant asked how far that monetary inflation would create inflationary pressure rather than treating every cause of rising prices as identical.
`经济讨论中，一位通胀政策支持者主张货币扩张思想及相应政策，另一位参与者则询问，这种货币膨胀在多大程度上会造成通胀压力，而没有把所有涨价原因都当成同一件事。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:inflation08 -->
Stuart jumped into the sea and inflated the liferaft.
`斯图尔特跳进海里，给救生筏充了气。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:inflation08 -->
Don's lifejacket had failed to inflate.
`唐的救生衣没能自动充气。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:inflation09 -->
The promotion of a big release can inflate a film's final cost.
`大片上映的宣传会抬高影片的最终成本。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:inflation09 -->
Clothing prices have not inflated as much as automobiles.
`服装价格的上涨没有汽车价格那么大。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:inflation10 -->
They inflated clients' medical treatment to defraud insurance companies.
`他们夸大客户接受治疗的情况来诈骗保险公司。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:inflation11 -->
Sam admitted that he had inflated the benefit and become inflated with pride in his own headline, whose inflated language now embarrassed him.
`山姆承认，自己夸大了好处，又为亲手写出的标题沾沾自喜，如今那种浮夸语言令他难堪。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:inflation12 -->
In the economics discussion, one inflationist defended inflationism and an inflationist policy that would inflate the currency, while another participant asked how far that monetary inflation would create inflationary pressure rather than treating every cause of rising prices as identical.
`经济讨论中，一位通胀政策支持者主张货币扩张思想及相应政策，另一位参与者则询问，这种货币膨胀在多大程度上会造成通胀压力，而没有把所有涨价原因都当成同一件事。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:inflation13 -->
They had to buy everything at inflated prices at the ranch store.
`他们不得不在牧场商店以虚高价格购买所有东西。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:inflation14 -->
He had spoken inflatedly about a tiny saving, and revising his opinion required him to remove that inflatedness before revising the estimated total.
`他曾浮夸地谈论一笔微小的节省，而要调整看法，他就得先去掉那种浮夸，再修正总额估计。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:inflation15 -->

<!-- WORD:W024 -->
# infrastructure

<!-- EXAMPLE:infrastructure01 -->
…investment in infrastructure.
`……对基础设施的投资。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:infrastructure02 -->
A reservist joined the reserve to reinforce the troops, and a quartermaster struggled to maintain the garrison from damaged military infrastructure until an order to withdraw arrived beside the model monitor in the shallow harbour.
`一名后备军人加入增援部队，军需官勉力利用受损的永久军事设施供给驻军，直到撤军命令传到浅水港口的战舰模型旁。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:infrastructure03 -->
That infrastructural question mattered more than the launch poster, especially after a burst pipe disrupted a plaster partition and forced them to protect the wiring behind it.
`这一基础设施问题比发布海报更重要，尤其是一根水管爆裂、冲裂石膏隔墙，迫使他们保护墙后的电线之后。`

<!-- WORD:W025 -->
# insure

<!-- EXAMPLE:insure01 -->
For protection against unforeseen emergencies, you insure your house and your car.
`为防范不可预见的紧急情况，你给住宅和汽车投保。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:insure01 -->
Think carefully before you insure against accident, sickness and redundancy.
`投保意外、疾病和裁员险之前，要仔细考虑。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:insure01 -->
We automatically insure your belongings against fire and theft.
`我们自动为你的个人物品投保火灾及盗窃险。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:insure01 -->
insured the house for £ 100,000
`给住宅投保十万英镑。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:insure01 -->
we have insured against flood damage
`我们已投保水灾损失险。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:insure02 -->
Before buying cover, Leena asked an insurer whether the centre could insure its equipment and whether the company would insure the stated activity, since insurable property did not settle the insurability of every proposed event.
`购买保障前，莉娜询问保险公司，中心能否为设备投保，以及公司是否愿意承保所述活动，因为财产可以投保，并不能确定每项计划活动都可承保。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:insure03 -->
He insured himself against failure by treating only people he was sure he could cure.
`他只治疗自己肯定能治好的患者，以此避免失败。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:insure03 -->
All the electronics in the world cannot insure against accidents, though.
`不过，世上所有电子设备也无法防止一切意外。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:insure03 -->
insured themselves against the rain by taking umbrellas
`他们带伞以防下雨。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:insure04 -->
The visitor brought an umbrella as insurance against rain and insured herself against a delay with a printed address, while the American helper said a second contact number would insure that a late caller could still reach someone.
`访客带伞以防雨，又用打印地址防备延误；那位美国帮手则说，第二个联系电话可以确保迟到的来电者仍能找到人。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:insure05 -->
Before buying cover, Leena asked an insurer whether the centre could insure its equipment and whether the company would insure the stated activity, since insurable property did not settle the insurability of every proposed event.
`购买保障前，莉娜询问保险公司，中心能否为设备投保，以及公司是否愿意承保所述活动，因为财产可以投保，并不能确定每项计划活动都可承保。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:insure06 -->

<!-- EXAMPLE:insure07 -->
The insurance company paid out for the stolen jewellery and silver.
`保险公司对被盗的珠宝和银器支付了赔偿。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:insure07 -->

`我们建议每次度假都购买旅行保险。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:insure08 -->

`这个国家需要防卫能力，以防备意外情况。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:insure08 -->
take an umbrella as insurance
`带把伞以防万一。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:insure09 -->

`草拟保单列明保障范围、应付的保险费和认可损失发生后可获的保险赔偿，因此没有人把保单、费用和可能赔款混为一谈。`

<!-- WORD:W026 -->
# interpret

<!-- EXAMPLE:interpret01 -->
The whole speech might well be interpreted as a coded message to the Americans.
`整篇演说很可能会被理解为向美国人传递的暗号。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:interpret01 -->
The judge quite rightly says that he has to interpret the law as it's been passed.
`法官很正确地说，他必须按法律通过时的文本来解释它。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:interpret01 -->
Both approaches agree on what is depicted in the poem, but not on how it should be interpreted.
`两种方法对诗中描写什么意见一致，但对应该怎样理解意见不同。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:interpret01 -->
interpreted his gesture as mocking
`把他的手势理解为嘲弄。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:interpret02 -->
The chambermaid spoke little English, so her husband came with her to interpret.
`客房女服务员英语说得很少，因此她丈夫陪她来做口译。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:interpret02 -->
Interpreters found they could not interpret half of what he said.
`口译员发现，他的话有一半无法翻译出来。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:interpret03 -->
The lead actor interpreted the final scene quietly, sustained his role throughout, and offered an interpretation shaped by an interpretative workshop and its interpretive notes; the pianist responded interpretatively to the speech, the singer approached it interpretively, and a little electronic sustain let the final note linger.
`主演安静地演绎最后一幕，始终充分保持角色，呈现了诠释工作坊及其解说笔记塑造的表演处理；钢琴手以演绎方式回应台词，歌手也从诠释角度处理它，而一点电子延音让最后的音符停留。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:interpret04 -->
An interpreter interpreted the visiting speaker's words for the audience, then asked for a clearer sentence whose interpretability would make the message interpretable without an unnecessary interpretational dispute.
`一名口译员为听众翻译来访讲者的话，随后请对方把一句话说得更清楚，使信息能够被理解，避免不必要的解释争议。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:interpret05 -->
An interpreter interpreted the visiting speaker's words for the audience, then asked for a clearer sentence whose interpretability would make the message interpretable without an unnecessary interpretational dispute.
`一名口译员为听众翻译来访讲者的话，随后请对方把一句话说得更清楚，使信息能够被理解，避免不必要的解释争议。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:interpret06 -->
The government put a different interpretation on the figures.
`政府对这些数字作出了另一种解读。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:interpret07 -->
…her full-bodied interpretation of the role of Micaela.
`……她对米凯拉这个角色饱满有力的演绎。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:interpret08 -->
An interpreter interpreted the visiting speaker's words for the audience, then asked for a clearer sentence whose interpretability would make the message interpretable without an unnecessary interpretational dispute.
`一名口译员为听众翻译来访讲者的话，随后请对方把一句话说得更清楚，使信息能够被理解，避免不必要的解释争议。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:interpret09 -->
History is an interpretive process.
`历史研究是一个诠释过程。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:interpret10 -->

<!-- EXAMPLE:interpret12 -->
Aristide spoke to the press through an interpreter.
`阿里斯蒂德通过口译员与媒体交谈。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:interpret13 -->
At the coding table, a different interpreter executed a short program line by line, and the children could see which instruction produced each printed result.
`编程桌旁，另一种解释程序逐行执行短程序，孩子们能够看清每条指令产生了什么打印结果。`

<!-- WORD:W027 -->
# liable

<!-- EXAMPLE:liable01 -->
When challenged about his behaviour, David was liable to dissolve in tears.
`每当有人质问大卫的行为，他很容易就泪流满面。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:liable01 -->
it is liable to rain
`很可能要下雨了。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:liable02 -->
She will grow into a woman particularly liable to depression.
`她长大后会成为特别容易陷入抑郁的女性。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:liable02 -->
This volcanic rock is less liable to shatter than limestone.
`这种火山岩比石灰岩更不易碎裂。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:liable03 -->
The airline's insurer is liable for damages to the victims' families.
`航空公司的保险方有义务向遇难者家属支付损害赔偿。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:liable03 -->
As the killings took place outside British jurisdiction, the Ministry of Defence could not be held liable.
`这些杀戮发生在英国司法管辖范围以外，因此不能认定国防部负有法律责任。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:liable04 -->
Their imaginary agreement made the centre liable for specified losses and liable to a penalty for a missed booking, so Leena asked a lawyer to explain that liability before anyone signed.
`他们设想的协议使中心须对特定损失负责，并可能因错过预约而承担罚金，因此莉娜请律师在任何人签字前解释这种责任。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:liable05 -->
He is claiming damages from London Underground, which has admitted liability but disputes the amount of his claim.
`他正在向伦敦地铁索赔，后者承认责任，但对索赔金额有异议。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:liable06 -->
Team-mates and coach began to see him as a liability.
`队友和教练开始把他看作累赘。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:liable06 -->
What was once a vote-catching policy is now a political liability.
`曾经能赢得选票的政策，现在成了政治负担。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:liable07 -->
The company had assets of $138 million and liabilities of $120.5 million.
`公司资产为一亿三千八百万美元，负债为一亿二千零五十万美元。`

<!-- WORD:W028 -->
# maintain

<!-- EXAMPLE:maintain01 -->
The Department maintains many close contacts with the chemical industry.
`该部门与化学工业界保持着许多密切联系。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:maintain01 -->
maintained friendly relations
`保持了友好关系。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:maintain02 -->
He has maintained that the money was donated for international purposes.
`他一直坚称那笔钱是为国际用途捐出的。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:maintain02 -->
'Not all feminism has to be like this,' Jo maintains.
`“并非所有女性主义都必须如此，”乔坚持说。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:maintain02 -->
He had always maintained his innocence.
`他一直坚称自己无罪。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:maintain02 -->
maintained that she was the best
`坚称她是最好的。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:maintain02 -->
his story was true, he maintained
`他坚持说，他的故事是真的。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:maintain03 -->
The government was right to maintain interest rates at a high level.
`政府把利率维持在高位是正确的。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:maintain04 -->
The house costs a fortune to maintain.
`这幢房子的维护要花一大笔钱。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:maintain04 -->
The cars are getting older and less well-maintained.
`这些汽车越来越旧，保养也越来越差。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:maintain05 -->
…the basic costs of maintaining a child.
`……抚养一个孩子的基本费用。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:maintain05 -->
maintained him in comfort
`让他过着舒适的生活。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:maintain05 -->
maintained themselves by fishing
`靠捕鱼维生。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:maintain06 -->
Mira maintained the cause of clear public information by rejecting a councillor's suggestion that the conditions were expendable details too unimportant to print.
`米拉支持清楚传播公共信息的事业，因而拒绝了一位委员的提议：他竟把条件说明视为无足轻重、可以不印的细节。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:maintain07 -->
A reservist joined the reserve to reinforce the troops, and a quartermaster struggled to maintain the garrison from damaged military infrastructure until an order to withdraw arrived beside the model monitor in the shallow harbour.
`一名后备军人加入增援部队，军需官勉力利用受损的永久军事设施供给驻军，直到撤军命令传到浅水港口的战舰模型旁。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:maintain08 -->
The aim was to maintain contact with visitors and maintain response times at an agreed level through a maintainable system whose maintainability did not depend on Jon alone.
`目标是通过一套可以维护的系统保持与访客的联系，把响应时间维持在约定水平，而其可维护性不应只依靠乔恩。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:maintain09 -->
The aim was to maintain contact with visitors and maintain response times at an agreed level through a maintainable system whose maintainability did not depend on Jon alone.
`目标是通过一套可以维护的系统保持与访客的联系，把响应时间维持在约定水平，而其可维护性不应只依靠乔恩。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:maintain10 -->
…maintenance work on government buildings.
`……政府建筑的维修工作。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:maintain10 -->
The window had been replaced last week during routine maintenance.
`那扇窗上周在例行维修时已经更换。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:maintain11 -->
…the government's plan to make absent fathers pay maintenance for their children.
`……政府要求不与孩子同住的父亲支付孩子抚养费的计划。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:maintain12 -->
…the maintenance of peace and stability in Asia.
`……维持亚洲的和平与稳定。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:maintain13 -->
Another character faced an old charge of maintenance for unlawfully supporting a lawsuit over an estate acquired by purchase rather than inheritance, and the dispute concerned a valuation at twenty years' purchase, twenty times its annual rent, and a reservation of grazing rights in the transfer.
`另一角色因非法支持一场诉讼而面对旧时包揽诉讼罪的指控；涉案地产由个人行为取得而非继承，争议涉及按二十年收益、即年租金二十倍计算的估值，以及转让时保留的放牧权。`

<!-- WORD:W029 -->
# mitigate

<!-- EXAMPLE:mitigate01 -->
…ways of mitigating the effects of an explosion.
`……减轻爆炸影响的办法。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:mitigate01 -->
your offer certainly mitigated their hostility
`你的提议确实减轻了他们的敌意。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:mitigate02 -->
Taking their own booking page offline would disrupt appointments, but a paper desk could mitigate the disruption, making some of the inconvenience mitigable while leaving the limits of that mitigation clear.
`把自己的预约网页下线会打乱预约，但纸质接待台能够减轻这种中断，使部分不便可以缓解，同时清楚说明这种缓解的限度。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:mitigate03 -->
…the mitigation or cure of a physical or mental condition.
`……减轻或治愈某种身心状况。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:mitigate04 -->
Kieran Coonan QC told the judge in mitigation that the offences had been at the lower end of the scale.
`御用大律师基兰·库南向法官说明，这些罪行属于较轻程度，以求减轻处罚。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:mitigate05 -->
A staffed desk could be a mitigator of confusion, and its mitigatory role mattered more than a display that flashed disruptively and added to the disruptiveness of an already disruptive queue.
`有人值守的接待台能够减少混乱，这种缓解作用比一个扰乱人心地闪烁、又加重本已混乱队伍之干扰程度的显示屏更有价值。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:mitigate06 -->
A staffed desk could be a mitigator of confusion, and its mitigatory role mattered more than a display that flashed disruptively and added to the disruptiveness of an already disruptive queue.
`有人值守的接待台能够减少混乱，这种缓解作用比一个扰乱人心地闪烁、又加重本已混乱队伍之干扰程度的显示屏更有价值。`

<!-- WORD:W030 -->
# monitor

<!-- EXAMPLE:monitor01 -->
Officials had not been allowed to monitor the voting.
`官员此前不获准监督投票。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:monitor01 -->
You need feedback to monitor progress.
`你需要反馈来监测进展。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:monitor02 -->
Peter Murray is in London and has been monitoring reports out of Monrovia.
`彼得·默里现于伦敦，一直在监听从蒙罗维亚发出的报道。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:monitor03 -->
The claim “The heart monitor shows low levels of consciousness” confuses cardiac activity with consciousness.
`“心脏监测仪显示意识水平很低”这一说法混淆了心脏活动与意识。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:monitor04 -->
He was watching a game of tennis on a television monitor.
`他正在电视监视器上看一场网球赛。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:monitor05 -->
Last night you went home without switching off your computer monitor.
`昨晚你回家时没有关电脑显示器。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:monitor06 -->
Government monitors will continue to accompany reporters.
`政府监督员将继续陪同记者。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:monitor07 -->
The open day began with a school monitor guiding visitors through the display, and the old monitorial system in one photograph made her reflect on the difference between her helpful monitorship and commanding other children.
`开放日由一名学生值日生带领来客参观展览开始；照片中的旧式学生监督制度，让她思考自己的协助职责与命令其他孩子之间的区别。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:monitor08 -->
A nature teacher put a photograph of a monitor beside one of a domestic dog, asking why a domesticable animal and a familiar pet were not the same category and how people domesticate species over time through domestication.
`一位自然课教师把巨蜥照片放在家犬照片旁，问大家：可以驯化的动物为何不等于熟悉的宠物，人类又怎样通过长期驯化使物种适应人类生活。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:monitor09 -->
A reservist joined the reserve to reinforce the troops, and a quartermaster struggled to maintain the garrison from damaged military infrastructure until an order to withdraw arrived beside the model monitor in the shallow harbour.
`一名后备军人加入增援部队，军需官勉力利用受损的永久军事设施供给驻军，直到撤军命令传到浅水港口的战舰模型旁。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:monitor10 -->
At the small broadcasting desk, an engineer monitored the signal's technical quality on a studio monitor while another monitor monitored a foreign radio bulletin for the language workshop.
`在小型播音台，一名工程师通过演播室监视器调控信号的技术质量，另一名监听员则为语言工作坊监听一段外国广播简报。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:monitor11 -->
…analysis and monitoring of the global environment.
`……全球环境的分析与监测。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:monitor12 -->
The open day began with a school monitor guiding visitors through the display, and the old monitorial system in one photograph made her reflect on the difference between her helpful monitorship and commanding other children.
`开放日由一名学生值日生带领来客参观展览开始；照片中的旧式学生监督制度，让她思考自己的协助职责与命令其他孩子之间的区别。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:monitor13 -->

<!-- WORD:W031 -->
# precaution

<!-- EXAMPLE:precaution01 -->
Could he not, just as a precaution, move to a place of safety?
`他难道不能仅为预防起见，搬到安全的地方吗？`

<!-- EXAMPLE:precaution01 -->
Extra safety precautions are essential in homes where older people live.
`老年人居住的家中必须有额外安全防范措施。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:precaution02 -->
In a private sexual-health conversation, a visitor asked about taking precautions and asked whether withdrawal before ejaculation was the same as the method named on her leaflet, so the educator clarified the terms without assuming that naming a method proved its effectiveness.
`私下的性健康交流中，一位访客询问避孕措施，并问射精前抽出的做法是否就是传单所列方法；教育者解释了术语，没有因为说出方法名称就认定其有效。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:precaution03 -->
The local administration says the curfew is a precautionary measure.
`地方当局说，宵禁是一项预防措施。`

<!-- WORD:W032 -->
# proportion

<!-- EXAMPLE:proportion01 -->
A large proportion of the dolphins in that area will eventually die.
`那个地区的大部分海豚最终都会死亡。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:proportion01 -->
A proportion of the rent is met by the city council.
`市议会负担部分租金。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:proportion01 -->
a large proportion of the profits
`利润的一大部分。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:proportion02 -->
The proportion of women in the profession had risen to 17.3%.
`该行业女性所占比例已升至百分之十七点三。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:proportion03 -->
Women's bodies tend to have a higher proportion of fat to water.
`女性身体的脂肪与水分之比往往更高。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:proportion03 -->
the proportion of births to deaths
`出生人数与死亡人数的比例。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:proportion04 -->
In the tropics plants grow to huge proportions.
`热带植物能长得非常巨大。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:proportion04 -->
large proportions
`巨大的尺寸。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:proportion05 -->
You can vary the relative proportions of things in a picture very simply.
`你可以很容易地改变画面中各事物的相对比例。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:proportion05 -->
the house has fine proportions
`这座房子各部分比例优美。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:proportion06 -->
The pressure in the cylinders would go up in proportion to the boiler pressure.
`汽缸内压力会随着锅炉压力按比例上升。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:proportion07 -->
Children have relatively larger heads than adults in proportion to the rest of their body.
`与身体其他部位相比，儿童的头比成年人的相对更大。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:proportion08 -->
The punishment was out of all proportion to the crime.
`惩罚与罪行完全不相称。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:proportion09 -->
Everything just got blown out of proportion.
`一切都被夸大了。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:proportion09 -->
exaggerated out of all proportion
`被极度夸大。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:proportion10 -->
We've got to keep this in proportion.
`我们必须恰如其分地看待这件事。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:proportion10 -->
must get the facts in proportion
`必须以恰当尺度看待这些事实。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:proportion11 -->
We must not lose our sense of proportion.
`我们不能失去判断轻重的分寸感。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:proportion12 -->
Using the rule of three, Sam completed the proportion 3:5 = 9:15 and identified each proportional, then used a different exercise in which the time needed for a fixed journey stood in inverse proportion to the constant speed chosen.
`山姆用比例法补全了三比五等于九比十五的比例式，辨认了每个比例项，又做了另一道题：固定路程中，所需时间与选取的恒定速度成反比。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:proportion13 -->
must proportion the punishment to the crime
`必须使惩罚与罪行相称。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:proportion14 -->
A well-proportioned model of the centre stood beside a proportionless first sketch, and the contrast in their proportions showed how a modest building could acquire monstrous proportions when a drawing ignored scale.
`中心的匀称模型旁边放着一张比例失调的初稿，两者各部分比例的对比显示：绘图若忽略尺度，一座不大的建筑也会显得庞大怪异。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:proportion15 -->
A well-proportioned model of the centre stood beside a proportionless first sketch, and the contrast in their proportions showed how a modest building could acquire monstrous proportions when a drawing ignored scale.
`中心的匀称模型旁边放着一张比例失调的初稿，两者各部分比例的对比显示：绘图若忽略尺度，一座不大的建筑也会显得庞大怪异。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:proportion16 -->
For the shared journey, they would proportion each contribution to the seats used, making the proportionment fair, testing its proportionality, and charging a proportionate amount so that everyone paid proportionately.
`对于合乘，他们会按使用座位数分摊款项，使按比例分配公平，检验其比例是否相称，再收取相称金额，让每个人都按比例付款。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:proportion17 -->
Loss of weight is directly proportional to the rate at which the disease is progressing.
`体重减轻与疾病进展速度成正比。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:proportion17 -->
a proportional increase in the expense
`开支按比例增加。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:proportion17 -->
resentment proportional to his injuries
`与他所受伤害相称的怨恨。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:proportion18 -->
Using the rule of three, Sam completed the proportion 3:5 = 9:15 and identified each proportional, then used a different exercise in which the time needed for a fixed journey stood in inverse proportion to the constant speed chosen.
`山姆用比例法补全了三比五等于九比十五的比例式，辨认了每个比例项，又做了另一道题：固定路程中，所需时间与选取的恒定速度成反比。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:proportion19 -->
For the shared journey, they would proportion each contribution to the seats used, making the proportionment fair, testing its proportionality, and charging a proportionate amount so that everyone paid proportionately.
`对于合乘，他们会按使用座位数分摊款项，使按比例分配公平，检验其比例是否相称，再收取相称金额，让每个人都按比例付款。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:proportion20 -->
You have proportionally more fat on your thighs and hips than anywhere else on your body.
`相较而言，你的大腿和臀部比身体其他部位含有更多脂肪。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:proportion21 -->
Republics will have voting rights proportionate to the size of their economies.
`各共和国将拥有与其经济规模成比例的投票权。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:proportion22 -->
Direct cost tends to vary proportionately with the level of output.
`直接成本往往随产出水平按比例变化。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:proportion23 -->
Only in a simplified worksheet did cost rise in proportion to identical baskets, making it directly proportional to quantity and in direct proportion to it; in proportion to the whole monthly budget, however, the saving remained proportionally small.
`只有在简化练习表中，同样的食品篮子的成本才会随数量按比例增加，即与数量成正比；然而，与整个月的预算相比，这笔节省在比例上仍然很小。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:proportion24 -->
Using the rule of three, Sam completed the proportion 3:5 = 9:15 and identified each proportional, then used a different exercise in which the time needed for a fixed journey stood in inverse proportion to the constant speed chosen.
`山姆用比例法补全了三比五等于九比十五的比例式，辨认了每个比例项，又做了另一道题：固定路程中，所需时间与选取的恒定速度成反比。`

<!-- WORD:W033 -->
# purchase

<!-- EXAMPLE:purchase01 -->
He purchased a ticket and went up on the top deck.
`他买了一张票，走上顶层甲板。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:purchase02 -->
Some of the receipts had been for the purchase of cars.
`有些收据是购车时取得的。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:purchase03 -->
She opened the tie box and looked at her purchase. It was silk, with maroon stripes.
`她打开领带盒，看着自己买的东西。那是丝质的，有栗色条纹。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:purchase04 -->
I got a purchase on the rope and pulled.
`我抓牢绳子，用力拉。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:purchase04 -->
I couldn't get any purchase with the screwdriver on the damn screws.
`螺丝刀怎么也咬不住那些该死的螺丝。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:purchase05 -->
They could not purchase public trust at the cost of hiding a weakness, and Sam was no longer secure of success simply because he had produced a persuasive poster.
`他们不能以隐瞒弱点为代价换取公众信任，山姆也不再仅因制作了有说服力的海报就确信能成功。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:purchase06 -->
At a model harbour, a child gained a purchase on a rope and used a small purchase to purchase the toy anchor, discovering that a yielding rope's yieldingness could absorb a little movement without making the knot safe by itself.
`在港口模型旁，一个孩子抓牢绳子，用小滑车组提起玩具船锚，发现柔软绳子的可变形性能够吸收一点位移，却不能单凭这一点就保证绳结安全。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:purchase07 -->
Another character faced an old charge of maintenance for unlawfully supporting a lawsuit over an estate acquired by purchase rather than inheritance, and the dispute concerned a valuation at twenty years' purchase, twenty times its annual rent, and a reservation of grazing rights in the transfer.
`另一角色因非法支持一场诉讼而面对旧时包揽诉讼罪的指控；涉案地产由个人行为取得而非继承，争议涉及按二十年收益、即年租金二十倍计算的估值，以及转让时保留的放牧权。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:purchase08 -->
At a model harbour, a child gained a purchase on a rope and used a small purchase to purchase the toy anchor, discovering that a yielding rope's yieldingness could absorb a little movement without making the knot safe by itself.
`在港口模型旁，一个孩子抓牢绳子，用小滑车组提起玩具船锚，发现柔软绳子的可变形性能够吸收一点位移，却不能单凭这一点就保证绳结安全。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:purchase09 -->
Another character faced an old charge of maintenance for unlawfully supporting a lawsuit over an estate acquired by purchase rather than inheritance, and the dispute concerned a valuation at twenty years' purchase, twenty times its annual rent, and a reservation of grazing rights in the transfer.
`另一角色因非法支持一场诉讼而面对旧时包揽诉讼罪的指控；涉案地产由个人行为取得而非继承，争议涉及按二十年收益、即年租金二十倍计算的估值，以及转让时保留的放牧权。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:purchase10 -->
For a replacement printer, the purchaser compared what was purchasable outright with hire purchase, then purchased one without instalments and displayed the purchase with its receipt as a record of the purchase.
`购买替换打印机时，购买者比较了可一次买下的设备与分期付款方案，随后一次付清买下一台，把所购物品与收据一起展示，作为购买记录。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:purchase11 -->
The broker will get 5% if he finds a purchaser.
`经纪人若找到买主，就会拿到百分之五。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:purchase12 -->
…buying a car on hire purchase.
`……以分期付款方式买车。`

<!-- WORD:W034 -->
# reliable

<!-- EXAMPLE:reliable01 -->
She was efficient and reliable.
`她高效而可靠。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reliable01 -->
Japanese cars are so reliable.
`日本汽车很可靠。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reliable02 -->
There is no reliable information about civilian casualties.
`关于平民伤亡没有可靠消息。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reliable03 -->
It's been working reliably for years.
`它多年来一直可靠地运作。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reliable04 -->
Sonia, we are reliably informed, loves her family very much.
`据可靠消息，索尼娅非常爱自己的家人。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reliable05 -->
He's not at all worried about his car's reliability.
`他一点也不担心汽车的可靠性。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reliable05 -->
Both questioned the reliability of recent opinion polls.
`两人都质疑近期民意调查的可靠性。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reliable06 -->
They relied heavily on the advice of their professional advisers.
`他们十分依赖专业顾问的意见。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reliable06 -->
relies on her for everything
`什么事都依赖她。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reliable07 -->
I know I can rely on you to sort it out.
`我知道可以信赖你把它解决。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reliable07 -->
The Red Cross are relying on us.
`红十字会正指望着我们。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reliable07 -->
am relying on your judgement
`正信赖你的判断。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reliable08 -->
…the country's increasing reliance on foreign aid.
`……这个国家对外援日益增加的依赖。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reliable08 -->
put full reliance in you
`完全信赖你。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reliable09 -->
They were reliably informed that a neighbour could lend a printer, and that neighbour's practical help became their chief reliance for the afternoon.
`他们从可靠消息得知一位邻居能借出打印机，而那位邻居的实际帮助成了他们下午最主要的依靠。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reliable10 -->
These people are not wholly reliant on Western charity.
`这些人并非完全依赖西方慈善援助。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reliable11 -->
She is intelligent and self-reliant, speaking her mind and not suffering fools gladly.
`她聪明自立，坦率表达意见，不愿对愚蠢之人委曲求全。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reliable12 -->
People learned self-reliance because they had to.
`人们因为迫不得已而学会自立。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reliable13 -->
He was self-reliant and worked self-reliantly, but Mira distinguished that useful self-reliance from an older man's self-sufficient manner as he refused to hear another opinion, while respecting a neighbour who was self-sufficient enough to enjoy living alone.
`他能自立，也独立地工作，但米拉把这种有益自立与另一位年长男子自负地拒听他人意见的态度区分开，同时尊重一位独立自足、能够享受独居生活的邻居。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reliable14 -->
Diplomats can be a notoriously unreliable and misleading source of information.
`外交官作为消息来源可能极不可靠且会误导人，这一点众所周知。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reliable14 -->
He had an unreliable car.
`他的汽车不可靠。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reliable15 -->
…his lateness and unreliability.
`……他的迟到和不可靠。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reliable16 -->
A reliable printer could work reliably for years, but this one fed sheets unreliably, and its unreliability made it an unreliable foundation for promises compared with the replacement's tested reliability.
`可靠的打印机可以多年稳定运作，但这一台进纸时好时坏；与替换设备经过检验的可靠性相比，它的不可靠使它无法成为承诺的良好基础。`

<!-- WORD:W035 -->
# reserve

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve01 -->
A double room with a balcony overlooking the sea had been reserved for him.
`已经为他留好一间带海景阳台的双人房。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve02 -->
I'll reserve a table for five.
`我会预订一张供五个人用餐的桌子。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve03 -->
The Gulf has 65 per cent of the world's oil reserves.
`海湾地区拥有全球百分之六十五的石油储量。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve03 -->
a great reserve of strength
`充足的力量储备。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve03 -->
huge energy reserves
`巨大的能量储备。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve04 -->
He ended up as a reserve, but still qualified for a team gold medal.
`他最后当了替补，但仍然获得了领取团体金牌的资格。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve05 -->
Marine biologists are calling for Cardigan Bay to be created a marine nature reserve to protect the dolphins.
`海洋生物学家呼吁将卡迪根湾划为海洋自然保护区来保护海豚。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve05 -->
nature reserve
`自然保护区。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve06 -->
His natural reserve made him appear self-conscious.
`他天生矜持，显得有些不自在。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve06 -->
difficult to overcome his reserve
`难以克服他的拘谨。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve07 -->
The company needs to keep some cash in reserve.
`公司需要留一些现金备用。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve08 -->
A reservist joined the reserve to reinforce the troops, and a quartermaster struggled to maintain the garrison from damaged military infrastructure until an order to withdraw arrived beside the model monitor in the shallow harbour.
`一名后备军人加入增援部队，军需官勉力利用受损的永久军事设施供给驻军，直到撤军命令传到浅水港口的战舰模型旁。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve09 -->
reserve the right to
`保留采取某行动的权利。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve09 -->
He reserved the right to change his mind.
`他保留了改变主意的权利。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve10 -->
reserved my comments until the end
`把自己的评论留到最后才说。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve10 -->
Doctors are reserving judgement on his ability to travel until later in the week.
`医生们要等到本周稍晚才判断他能否旅行。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve11 -->
accept your offer without reserve
`无条件接受你们的提议。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve12 -->
Her credibility as an artist had grown through years of thoughtful work, and the reserve in her performance made the governor's cruelty more powerful than a shouted speech would have done.
`她多年来凭认真作品积累了艺术声望，而表演中的克制，让州长的残酷比高喊台词更有力量。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve13 -->
The model accounts distinguished a reserve created from retained company profits, liquid reserves held for emergencies, a holding in a reserve currency and a physical reserve of spare paper, so useful buffers did not become one imaginary pot of money.
`模型账目区分了由公司留存利润形成的准备金、应急流动储备资产、储备货币持有额，以及实际储存的备用纸张，避免把这些有用缓冲混成一笔想象中的钱。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve14 -->
reserve currency
`储备货币。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve15 -->
Indian reserve
`原住民保留地。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve16 -->
exercised a certain amount of reserve
`有意隐瞒了一部分情况。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve17 -->
A textile worker cut a bias across the weave, laid the fabric on the bias and protected a pale reserve from dye, turning three easily confused descriptions into three visible decisions in one repair.
`一名纺织工斜穿经纬裁出一条斜边，把布料斜向铺放，并保护一块浅色留白区不受染色，让三个容易混淆的描述成为同一次修补中的三个可见决定。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve18 -->
Beyond the stage, a visitor showed a family map of a reserve in one country and a reservation in another, asking the pupils to present an uncertain detail with all reserve instead of exercising reserve to conceal the gaps in their knowledge.
`舞台之外，一位访客展示家族地图，上面标着一个国家的原住民保留地和另一个国家的保留区；他请学生对未确定的细节充分保留，而不要有意隐瞒知识中的缺口。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve19 -->
Sam, now the group's reserver of rooms, reserved a table, checked which seats were reservable, and made a reservation while keeping two places in reserve for the people for whom they had been reserved.
`山姆如今负责替团队订房订位，他预订了一张桌子，检查哪些座位可以预订，并办妥预约，同时把两个已为特定来客留好的名额备用。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve20 -->
Sam, now the group's reserver of rooms, reserved a table, checked which seats were reservable, and made a reservation while keeping two places in reserve for the people for whom they had been reserved.
`山姆如今负责替团队订房订位，他预订了一张桌子，检查哪些座位可以预订，并办妥预约，同时把两个已为特定来客留好的名额备用。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve21 -->
I told him my main reservation about his film was the ending.
`我告诉他，我对那部电影最主要的保留意见在于结尾。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve21 -->
had reservations about the plan
`对那个计划抱有保留意见。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve22 -->
He went to the desk to make a reservation.
`他去服务台作了预订。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve23 -->
Seventeen thousand Native Americans live on this reservation.
`一万七千名美国原住民住在这片保留地。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve24 -->
A visiting student had paid a retainer to keep her room during an absence, and the reservation of that room meant it stayed reserved for her, although she joked that the evening's last washing-up duty seemed reserved for her as well.
`一位来访学生在离开期间付了留房定金，因此房间继续为她保留；不过她开玩笑说，晚上最后一轮洗碗工作似乎也注定留给她。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve25 -->
Outside, the American visitor reminded a driver to yield to pedestrians, and the route map showed a central reservation that meant the group had to cross at the designated point rather than straight from the gate.
`外面，那位美国访客提醒司机让行人先走；路线图标出的中央隔离带，也意味着团队必须到指定地点过街，不能从大门径直穿过去。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve26 -->
Another character faced an old charge of maintenance for unlawfully supporting a lawsuit over an estate acquired by purchase rather than inheritance, and the dispute concerned a valuation at twenty years' purchase, twenty times its annual rent, and a reservation of grazing rights in the transfer.
`另一角色因非法支持一场诉讼而面对旧时包揽诉讼罪的指控；涉案地产由个人行为取得而非继承，争议涉及按二十年收益、即年租金二十倍计算的估值，以及转让时保留的放牧权。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve27 -->
For the church-history display, the curator placed a vessel used in reservation of the Eucharist beside documents about reservation of absolution to a superior and reservation of appointments to the Pope, then set an image of the Assumption next to a calendar marking the Assumption on 15 August as a Catholic feast.
`在教会历史展中，策展人把用于保留圣餐的器皿，放在涉及向高级教职者保留赦罪权、向教皇保留任命权的文件旁，再把圣母升天图像放在日历边，日历将八月十五日标为天主教圣母升天节。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve28 -->

`在教会历史展中，策展人把用于保留圣餐的器皿，放在涉及向高级教职者保留赦罪权、向教皇保留任命权的文件旁，再把圣母升天图像放在日历边，日历将八月十五日标为天主教圣母升天节。`

`在教会历史展中，策展人把用于保留圣餐的器皿，放在涉及向高级教职者保留赦罪权、向教皇保留任命权的文件旁，再把圣母升天图像放在日历边，日历将八月十五日标为天主教圣母升天节。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve30 -->

`他不露感情，相当安静，而且矜持。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve31 -->
Seats, or sometimes entire tables, were reserved.
`一些座位，有时整张桌子，都已被预订。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve32 -->

<!-- EXAMPLE:reserve33 -->

<!-- WORD:W036 -->
# resilient

<!-- EXAMPLE:resilient01 -->
…an armchair of some resilient plastic material.
`……一把用有弹性的塑料材料制作的扶手椅。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:resilient02 -->
When the U.S. stock market collapsed in October 1987, the Japanese stock market was the most resilient.
`一九八七年十月美国股市崩盘时，日本股市的恢复能力最强。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:resilient03 -->
Do your muscles have the strength and resilience that they should have?
`你的肌肉具有应有的力量和弹性吗？`

<!-- EXAMPLE:resilient03 -->
…the resilience of human beings to fight after they've been attacked.
`……人类在遭受袭击后继续抗争的韧性。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:resilient04 -->
The replacement printer stood on resilient pads whose resilience let them recover after compression, while Sam watched a compressed sample spring resiliently back into shape.
`替换的打印机放在有回弹力的垫子上，垫子的弹性使它们在受压后能够复原；山姆还看到一块受压试样有弹性地恢复原形。`

<!-- WORD:W037 -->
# restrict

<!-- EXAMPLE:restrict01 -->
There is talk of restricting the number of students on campus.
`有人讨论限制校园内的学生人数。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:restrict01 -->
…restricting imports to 0.6 billion pounds of sugar per year.
`……把糖的年进口量限制为六亿磅。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:restrict01 -->
restricted parking
`受限制的停车安排。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:restrict02 -->
The government imprisoned dissidents, forbade travel, and restricted the press.
`政府监禁异议者，禁止旅行，并限制新闻界。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:restrict03 -->
For the first two weeks patients are restricted to the grounds.
`最初两周，患者只能在院内活动。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:restrict03 -->
restricted them to five days a week
`把他们的工作时间限定为每周五天。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:restrict04 -->
The hospital may restrict bookings to people living locally.
`医院可能只接受本地居民预约。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:restrict05 -->
They would not restrict anyone's right to question it, although they would restrict circulation of the emergency contact sheet, share it restrictively, and keep the restricted list in a restricted office whose use was restricted to the leaders.
`他们不会限制任何人质疑规定的权利，但会限制紧急联络表的传播，按限定范围分享，把这份有限名单存放在限制进入的办公室中，而办公室只供带队者使用。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:restrict06 -->
Sam thought the information was being circulated too restrictedly, while a restrictionist on the committee defended a restrictionist policy, so Mira asked them to compare the actual risks instead of defending restrictedness for its own sake.
`山姆觉得资料传播得过于受限，而委员会中一位支持限制的人为限制政策辩护，于是米拉请双方比较实际风险，不要为了限制本身而维护受限状态。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:restrict07 -->
Sam thought the information was being circulated too restrictedly, while a restrictionist on the committee defended a restrictionist policy, so Mira asked them to compare the actual risks instead of defending restrictedness for its own sake.
`山姆觉得资料传播得过于受限，而委员会中一位支持限制的人为限制政策辩护，于是米拉请双方比较实际风险，不要为了限制本身而维护受限状态。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:restrict08 -->
…the lifting of restrictions on political parties and the news media.
`……取消对政党和新闻媒体的限制。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:restrict09 -->
Some restriction on funding was necessary.
`有必要对经费作一些限制。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:restrict09 -->
…the justification for this restriction of individual liberty.
`……为限制个人自由所作的辩护。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:restrict09 -->
His parents are trying to make up to him for the restrictions of urban living.
`他的父母想弥补城市生活给他带来的局限。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:restrict10 -->

`英国将采取限制更多的军火销售政策。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:restrict13 -->

<!-- EXAMPLE:restrict14 -->

`一些访客不喜欢名额限制，但陪同人员不足确实构成制约，因此带队者说明了这项限制性规定，没有掩饰它限制选择的程度。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:restrict15 -->
…the monotony of a heavily restricted diet.
`……严格限食所带来的单调。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:restrict16 -->

`纪律问题绝非只限于依靠福利生活家庭中的孩子。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:restrict17 -->

`……旧霍丁卡机场附近一处严格限制进入的区域。`

<!-- WORD:W038 -->
# retain

<!-- EXAMPLE:retain01 -->
The interior of the shop still retains a nineteenth-century atmosphere.
`店内仍保留着十九世纪的氛围。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:retain02 -->
He decided to retain him for the trial.
`他决定聘定他代理这场审判。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:retain03 -->
To work transparently, they retained the comparison's boundaries in a small box whose transparency helped readers retain the difference between a price level and a change in prices.
`为了公开明白地工作，他们在一个小框中保留比较的边界；框中文字的清楚明白，能帮助读者记住价格水平与价格变化之间的区别。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:retain04 -->
The model bridge could sustain a measured weight, while a retainer held its pin in place and a soil tray retained water because its mixture was retentive of moisture, a retentiveness that the dry sand beside it lacked.
`模型桥能支撑一定重量，固定件让插销保持原位，土盘则因混合土能保持水分而留住了水，这种保水能力是旁边干沙所没有的。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:retain05 -->
They retained a lawyer on a modest retainer to check which documents were retainable and how long their retainability mattered, recording the retainment of the signed agreement separately from its public summary.
`他们支付一笔不高的聘金，请律师检查哪些文件可以保留，以及保留资格在多长时间内有意义，并把签字协议的留存与公开摘要分开记录。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:retain06 -->

`他们支持保留强有力的中央政府。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:retain09 -->

`模拟病例记录区分了肾功能不全与尿潴留，描述用于固定、压住血管的结扎材料，并另附支撑牙桥的牙科固位体图示，而没有把展览变成治疗操作指引。`

<!-- WORD:W039 -->
# revise

<!-- EXAMPLE:revise01 -->
He soon came to revise his opinion of the profession.
`他很快改变了对这个职业的看法。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:revise02 -->
Some of their prices were higher than their competitors' so they revised their prices accordingly.
`他们的部分价格高于竞争者，因此作了相应调整。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:revise03 -->
Three editors handled the work of revising the articles for publication.
`三位编辑负责修改这些文章，以供出版。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:revise03 -->
The staff should work together to revise the school curriculum.
`教职员工应一起修订学校课程。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:revise04 -->
I have to revise for maths.
`我得复习数学。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:revise04 -->
I'd better skip the party and stay at home to revise.
`我最好不去聚会，留在家里复习。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:revise05 -->
As the day declined, the printer delivered a revise incorporating the earlier corrections, and even the committee's habitual decliner of every new suggestion accepted the cleaner page.
`一天将近结束时，印刷人员送来纳入前次校正的改样，连委员会中那位总拒绝新建议的人也接受了更清楚的版面。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:revise06 -->
Sam's revisable notice went to Leena as reviser, and after her revision of the conditions, the new revision promised a process for checking assistance rather than assistance without conditions.
`山姆那份可以修订的告示交给莉娜审订；她修改条件后，新修订本承诺的是核查协助服务的流程，而非无条件的协助。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:revise07 -->
The committee's revisory meeting accepted that revisal, and a revisionary clause allowed the plan to change if the relevant facts changed.
`委员会的审订会议接受了这次修订，一项允许修订的条款规定，相关事实改变时，计划也可以改变。`
