<!-- WORD:W040 -->
# risk

<!-- EXAMPLE:risk01 -->
There is a small risk of brain damage from the procedure.
`这项操作存在较小的脑损伤风险。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:risk01 -->
In all the confusion, there's a serious risk that the main issues will be forgotten.
`混乱之中，主要问题很可能被遗忘。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:risk01 -->
a risk of fire
`起火风险。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:risk02 -->
You're taking a big risk showing this to Kravis.
`把这个给克拉维斯看，你可是在冒很大的风险。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:risk03 -->
It's being overfat that constitutes a health risk.
`身体脂肪过多才是构成健康风险的因素。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:risk03 -->
The restaurant has been refurbished–it was found to be a fire risk.
`餐馆已重新整修，因为此前发现它有火灾危险。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:risk03 -->
a health risk
`健康危险因素。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:risk03 -->
is a poor risk
`是高风险患者。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:risk04 -->
Before providing the cash, they will have to decide whether you are a good or bad risk.
`提供现金前，他们必须判断你是信用风险较低还是较高的借款人。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:risk05 -->
Those who fail to register risk severe penalties.
`没有登记的人可能面临严厉处罚。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:risk05 -->
could not risk getting wet
`不能冒被淋湿的风险。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:risk06 -->
The captain was not willing to risk taking his ship through the straits in such bad weather.
`船长不愿冒险，在如此恶劣的天气中驾船穿越海峡。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:risk07 -->
She risked her own life to help a disabled woman.
`她冒着生命危险去帮助一位残障女性。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:risk08 -->
Up to 25,000 jobs are still at risk.
`仍有多达两万五千个工作岗位处于危险中。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:risk09 -->
At the risk of being repetitive, I will say again that statistics are only a guide.
`即使可能显得重复，我仍要再次说，统计数据只是参考。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:risk10 -->
Those who wish to come here will do so at their own risk.
`想来这里的人须自行承担风险。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:risk11 -->
The officers had run the risk of being dismissed.
`这些军官冒着被解职的风险。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:risk12 -->
Investing in airlines is a very risky business.
`投资航空公司是风险很高的生意。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:risk13 -->
One pupil suggested a risky joke with a mildly indecent double meaning, but the cast removed it because its riskiness lay in a sexual hint that would have distracted this audience from the messenger's plight.
`一名学生提议讲个带轻微荤意双关的笑话，但演员们删掉了它，因为它的不雅在于性暗示，会使这群观众分心，忽略信使的处境。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:risk14 -->
Operating riskily was not a sign of courage, and the riskiness of a shortcut made that risky option unacceptable while a safer one was available.
`冒险操作并不是勇敢的标志，而既然有更安全的办法可用，捷径所具有的危险性就使这一高风险选项无法接受。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:risk15 -->
Operating riskily was not a sign of courage, and the riskiness of a shortcut made that risky option unacceptable while a safer one was available.
`冒险操作并不是勇敢的标志，而既然有更安全的办法可用，捷径所具有的危险性就使这一高风险选项无法接受。`

<!-- WORD:W041 -->
# secure

<!-- EXAMPLE:secure01 -->
Federal leaders continued their efforts to secure a ceasefire.
`联邦领导人继续努力促成停火。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:secure01 -->
Graham's achievements helped secure him the job.
`格雷厄姆的成就帮助他获得了这份工作。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:secure01 -->
have secured front seats
`已经订到前排座位。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:secure02 -->
Staff withdrew from the main part of the prison but secured the perimeter.
`工作人员撤出了监狱主体区域，但守住了外围。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:secure03 -->
We shall make sure our home is as secure as possible from now on.
`从今以后，我们会尽可能确保住宅的安全。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:secure03 -->
secure against attack
`有充分防护，不易遭攻击。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:secure04 -->
He helped her close the cases up, and then he secured the canvas straps.
`他帮她合上箱子，随后系紧帆布带。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:secure04 -->
made the door secure
`把门关牢了。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:secure05 -->
Check joints are secure and the wood is sound.
`检查接头是否牢固，木料是否完好。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:secure06 -->
…trade union demands for secure wages and employment.
`……工会要求工资和就业得到稳定保障。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:secure06 -->
…the failure of financial institutions once thought to be secure.
`……那些一度被认为稳固的金融机构的倒闭。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:secure06 -->
He was determined to give his family a secure and solid base.
`他决意给家人一个稳定可靠的基础。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:secure06 -->
the plan is secure
`这个计划稳妥可靠。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:secure07 -->
She felt secure and protected when she was with him.
`与他在一起时，她感到安心，受到保护。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:secure08 -->
The loan is secured against your home.
`这笔贷款以你的住宅作担保。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:secure08 -->
His main task is to raise enough finance to repay secured loans.
`他的主要任务是筹足资金偿还抵押贷款。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:secure08 -->
a loan secured by property
`一笔以财产作担保的贷款。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:secure09 -->
secure of victory
`确信能获胜。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:secure10 -->
Not every entrance was securable with the available staff, and security accepted only a helper judged secure for the key-holding role, so an unused room stayed closed.
`现有人手无法保护每个入口，而保安部门只接受经判断适合安全持钥的帮手，因此一间不用的房间继续关闭。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:secure11 -->
The simulated case notes distinguished renal insufficiency from urinary retention, described a retentive ligature used to secure a blood vessel, and separately pictured a dental retainer supporting a bridge, without turning the display into instructions for treatment.
`模拟病例记录区分了肾功能不全与尿潴留，描述用于固定、压住血管的结扎材料，并另附支撑牙桥的牙科固位体图示，而没有把展览变成治疗操作指引。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:secure12 -->
An exempt commanded the royal guard, a noble's retainer carried a message, and the actor playing an old retainer teased his lifelong friend while the guard performed secure arms with wooden rifles to keep their locks from the imaginary rain.
`一位王室仪仗队指挥官指挥卫队，一名贵族家臣传递消息，而扮演忠实老友的演员打趣着相交一生的朋友；卫兵用木制步枪表演倒挟枪姿势，保护枪机免遭想象中的雨水。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:secure13 -->
Not every entrance was securable with the available staff, and security accepted only a helper judged secure for the key-holding role, so an unused room stayed closed.
`现有人手无法保护每个入口，而保安部门只接受经判断适合安全持钥的帮手，因此一间不用的房间继续关闭。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:secure14 -->
He locked the heavy door securely and kept the key in his pocket.
`他把沉重的门牢牢锁好，钥匙留在口袋里。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:secure15 -->
Ensure that the frame is securely fixed to the ground with bolts.
`确保框架用螺栓牢固固定在地面上。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:secure16 -->
Jon made the cupboard secure, secured the cable, checked that the bracket was secure, stored the spare tablet securely and fixed its mount securely, recording the securement before reopening the desk.
`乔恩把柜子锁好，固定电缆，检查支架是否牢固，把备用平板安全存放，再把它的底座牢牢固定，记录好固定工作后才重新开放接待台。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:secure17 -->
They are now under a great deal of pressure to tighten their airport security.
`他们现在承受着收紧机场保安措施的巨大压力。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:secure17 -->
Strict security measures are in force in the capital.
`首都实施着严格的保安措施。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:secure18 -->
He loves the security of a happy home life.
`他喜爱幸福家庭生活带来的安全感。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:secure19 -->
Wearing helmets gave cyclists a false sense of security and encouraged them to take risks.
`戴头盔让这些骑车人产生虚假的安全感，并鼓励他们冒险。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:secure20 -->
The central bank will provide special loans, and the banks will pledge the land as security.
`中央银行将提供特别贷款，银行会以土地作为抵押物。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:secure21 -->
…U.S. government securities and bonds.
`……美国政府证券和债券。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:secure22 -->
Not every entrance was securable with the available staff, and security accepted only a helper judged secure for the key-holding role, so an unused room stayed closed.
`现有人手无法保护每个入口，而保安部门只接受经判断适合安全持钥的帮手，因此一间不用的房间继续关闭。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:secure23 -->
…women who did not have jobs and were on social security.
`……没有工作、领取社会保障援助的女性。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:secure24 -->
Most mothers are insecure about their performance as mothers.
`多数母亲对自己作为母亲的表现缺乏信心。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:secure25 -->
…low-paid, insecure jobs.
`……低薪而不稳定的工作。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:secure26 -->
Sam was insecure about answering questions, and an insecure shelf fitted insecurely above the leaflets made him speak even more insecurely until Mira addressed both his insecurity and the loose fittings.
`山姆对回答问题缺乏信心，传单上方的架子又安装不牢，这让他说话更不安，直到米拉同时处理他的不安和松动的固定件。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:secure27 -->
She is always assailed by self-doubt and emotional insecurity.
`她总受到自我怀疑和情感不安的侵扰。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:secure27 -->
Crime creates feelings of insecurity in the population.
`犯罪使民众产生不安全感。`

<!-- WORD:W042 -->
# subsidy

<!-- EXAMPLE:subsidy01 -->
European farmers are planning a massive demonstration against farm subsidy cuts.
`欧洲农民正计划举行大规模示威，反对削减农业补贴。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:subsidy01 -->
housing subsidy
`住房补贴。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:subsidy02 -->
At lunch, Leena proposed a fictional transport subsidy, a charity's subsidy for advice sessions, and two distinct choices: subsidize a passenger's fare or subsidize the small service so that it could continue running.
`午饭时，莉娜提出一个虚构的交通补贴方案和慈善机构对咨询课的资助，再区分两种选择：补贴乘客票价，或者资助这项小型服务，使它能够持续经营。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:subsidy03 -->
The invented kingdom paid a subsidy to an ally for military aid, requested a subsidy from parliament for the sovereign's needs and levied a special subsidy for the campaign, while the pupils kept the three transactions separate in the prop accounts.
`这个虚构王国向盟国支付换取军事援助的款项，向议会申请满足君主国家需要的特别拨款，又为战役征收特别税；学生们在道具账目中把三笔交易分开。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:subsidy04 -->
The invented kingdom paid a subsidy to an ally for military aid, requested a subsidy from parliament for the sovereign's needs and levied a special subsidy for the campaign, while the pupils kept the three transactions separate in the prop accounts.
`这个虚构王国向盟国支付换取军事援助的款项，向议会申请满足君主国家需要的特别拨款，又为战役征收特别税；学生们在道具账目中把三笔交易分开。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:subsidy05 -->
The invented kingdom paid a subsidy to an ally for military aid, requested a subsidy from parliament for the sovereign's needs and levied a special subsidy for the campaign, while the pupils kept the three transactions separate in the prop accounts.
`这个虚构王国向盟国支付换取军事援助的款项，向议会申请满足君主国家需要的特别拨款，又为战役征收特别税；学生们在道具账目中把三笔交易分开。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:subsidy06 -->
At the moment they are existing on pensions that are subsidised by the government.
`目前他们靠政府补助的养老金维生。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:subsidy07 -->
The government continues to subsidize the production of eggs.
`政府继续补贴鸡蛋生产。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:subsidy07 -->
…a government decision to subsidise coal mining.
`……政府补助煤炭开采的决定。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:subsidy08 -->
…heavily subsidized prices for housing, bread, and meat.
`……住房、面包和肉类获得大量补贴后的价格。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:subsidy08 -->
…Scotland's subsidised theatre.
`……苏格兰获得补助的剧院。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:subsidy08 -->
subsidized lunches
`有补贴的午餐。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:subsidy09 -->
…the subsidising of alternative energy sources.
`……对替代能源的补贴。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:subsidy10 -->
…the federal government's subsidisation of poorer parts of the country.
`……联邦政府对国家较贫困地区的补助。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:subsidy11 -->
The subsidizer would have to show how subsidized fares differed from subsidised operating costs, because subsidizing a journey and subsidising an operator involved different questions about subsidization, or subsidisation in the other spelling.
`补贴方必须说明，受补贴的票价与获得资助的运营成本有何不同，因为补贴行程和资助运营者，涉及补贴过程中的不同问题。`

<!-- WORD:W043 -->
# sufficient

<!-- EXAMPLE:sufficient01 -->
One metre of fabric is sufficient to cover the exterior of an 18-in-diameter hatbox.
`一米布料足以包住直径十八英寸的帽盒外部。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sufficient01 -->
There was not sufficient evidence to secure a conviction.
`证据不足以定罪。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sufficient01 -->
sufficient food to feed her family
`足够养活全家的食物。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sufficient01 -->
sufficient reason for action
`采取行动的充分理由。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sufficient01 -->
wave of discontent sufficient to bring down the government
`足以使政府下台的不满浪潮。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sufficient02 -->
She recovered sufficiently to accompany him on his tour of Africa.
`她恢复到足以陪他去非洲巡游的程度。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sufficient03 -->
There's a sufficiency of drama in these lives to sustain your interest.
`这些人的生活中有足够戏剧性，能够持续吸引你的兴趣。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sufficient04 -->
In an old diary exercise, a writer in the decline of life feared that the library would fall into decline, called his own strength in decline, described a relative's illness as a decline, and praised a steward's sufficiency in managing the estate.
`在旧日记阅读练习中，一位晚年作者担忧图书馆开始衰落，写自己的体力正在减退，把亲人的消耗性疾病称为decline，又赞扬管家的办事能力。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sufficient05 -->
A cover letter should never exceed one page; often a far shorter letter will suffice.
`求职附信不应超过一页，通常远短于一页也就足够了。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sufficient05 -->
that will suffice for our purpose
`那足以满足我们的目的。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sufficient05 -->
suffices to prove it
`足以证明它。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sufficient05 -->
six sufficed him
`六个就满足了他的需要。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sufficient06 -->
Suffice it to say that afterwards we never met again.
`只需说，从那以后我们再未见面。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sufficient07 -->
He decided there was insufficient evidence to justify criminal proceedings.
`他认为证据不足以支持提起刑事诉讼。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sufficient08 -->
Food that is insufficiently cooked can lead to food poisoning.
`未充分煮熟的食物可能导致食物中毒。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sufficient09 -->
Late miscarriages are usually not due to hormonal insufficiency.
`晚期流产通常并非由于激素不足。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sufficient10 -->
renal insufficiency
`肾功能不全。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sufficient11 -->
This enabled the country to become self-sufficient in sugar.
`这使该国能够在食糖方面自给自足。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sufficient11 -->
Using traditional methods poor farmers can be virtually self-sufficient.
`采用传统方法，贫困农民也可以基本自给自足。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sufficient12 -->
Although she had various boyfriends, she was, and remains, fiercely self-sufficient.
`虽然她曾有不同男友，她过去和现在都非常独立自足。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sufficient13 -->
He was self-reliant and worked self-reliantly, but Mira distinguished that useful self-reliance from an older man's self-sufficient manner as he refused to hear another opinion, while respecting a neighbour who was self-sufficient enough to enjoy living alone.
`他能自立，也独立地工作，但米拉把这种有益自立与另一位年长男子自负地拒听他人意见的态度区分开，同时尊重一位独立自足、能够享受独居生活的邻居。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sufficient14 -->
The gardener hoped to become self-sufficient in seedlings, yet treated self-sufficiency as a limited goal, raising some plants self-sufficiently in a self-sufficing nursery while accepting help with heavy work.
`园丁希望育苗能够自给自足，却把这种自给视为有限目标：在能满足自身需要的苗圃中自行培育一部分植物，同时接受重活方面的帮助。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sufficient15 -->
The gardener hoped to become self-sufficient in seedlings, yet treated self-sufficiency as a limited goal, raising some plants self-sufficiently in a self-sufficing nursery while accepting help with heavy work.
`园丁希望育苗能够自给自足，却把这种自给视为有限目标：在能满足自身需要的苗圃中自行培育一部分植物，同时接受重活方面的帮助。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sufficient16 -->
The gardener hoped to become self-sufficient in seedlings, yet treated self-sufficiency as a limited goal, raising some plants self-sufficiently in a self-sufficing nursery while accepting help with heavy work.
`园丁希望育苗能够自给自足，却把这种自给视为有限目标：在能满足自身需要的苗圃中自行培育一部分植物，同时接受重活方面的帮助。`

<!-- WORD:W044 -->
# sustain

<!-- EXAMPLE:sustain01 -->
But he has sustained his fierce social conscience from young adulthood through old age.
`但是从青年时代直到老年，他一直保持强烈的社会良知。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sustain02 -->
Every aircraft in there has sustained some damage.
`里面的每架飞机都遭受了一些损坏。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sustain02 -->
A tourist died of injuries sustained in the bomb blast.
`一位游客因炸弹爆炸中所受的伤而死亡。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sustain03 -->
The cash dividends they get from the cash crop would sustain them during the lean season.
`经济作物带来的现金收益能支持他们度过青黄不接的季节。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sustain04 -->
The model bridge could sustain a measured weight, while a retainer held its pin in place and a soil tray retained water because its mixture was retentive of moisture, a retentiveness that the dry sand beside it lacked.
`模型桥能支撑一定重量，固定件让插销保持原位，土盘则因混合土能保持水分而留住了水，这种保水能力是旁边干沙所没有的。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sustain05 -->
The soup would sustain the helpers until evening and provide daily sustenance, but its maker earned her sustenance from paid catering, and a sufficiency of food depended on counting guests rather than guessing.
`汤能为帮手们维持体力到傍晚，提供日常滋养，但制作者靠有偿餐饮谋生；要有足量食物，就得清点来客，而不是猜测。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sustain06 -->
“I yield to none in wanting this room to work,” Mira said in the closing debate, then yielded to Sam so that he could speak, showing that a committee could sustain disagreement without silencing a new voice.
`“说到希望告示室运作好，我不逊于任何人，”米拉在闭幕讨论中说，随后把发言权让给山姆，表明委员会能够承受分歧，而不必压住新声音。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sustain07 -->
At a rehearsal for the afternoon's mock hearing, a clerk would call a witness in evidence to give evidence, ask her to verify her written statement on oath, and let the judge sustain an objection if the evidence failed to sustain a charge, before hearing anything offered in mitigation.
`在为下午模拟听证而作的排练中，书记员会传唤证人作证，请她以宣誓方式证实书面陈述；若证据无法支持指控，法官便可认可异议，然后再听取任何减轻情节的说明。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sustain08 -->
At a rehearsal for the afternoon's mock hearing, a clerk would call a witness in evidence to give evidence, ask her to verify her written statement on oath, and let the judge sustain an objection if the evidence failed to sustain a charge, before hearing anything offered in mitigation.
`在为下午模拟听证而作的排练中，书记员会传唤证人作证，请她以宣誓方式证实书面陈述；若证据无法支持指控，法官便可认可异议，然后再听取任何减轻情节的说明。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sustain09 -->
The lead actor interpreted the final scene quietly, sustained his role throughout, and offered an interpretation shaped by an interpretative workshop and its interpretive notes; the pianist responded interpretatively to the speech, the singer approached it interpretively, and a little electronic sustain let the final note linger.
`主演安静地演绎最后一幕，始终充分保持角色，呈现了诠释工作坊及其解说笔记塑造的表演处理；钢琴手以演绎方式回应台词，歌手也从诠释角度处理它，而一点电子延音让最后的音符停留。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sustain10 -->
The lead actor interpreted the final scene quietly, sustained his role throughout, and offered an interpretation shaped by an interpretative workshop and its interpretive notes; the pianist responded interpretatively to the speech, the singer approached it interpretively, and a little electronic sustain let the final note linger.
`主演安静地演绎最后一幕，始终充分保持角色，呈现了诠释工作坊及其解说笔记塑造的表演处理；钢琴手以演绎方式回应台词，歌手也从诠释角度处理它，而一点电子延音让最后的音符停留。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sustain11 -->
…a period of sustained economic growth throughout 1995.
`……一九九五年全年持续经济增长的时期。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sustain12 -->
The staff needed sustained effort to sustain the desk, and no single sustainer could work sustainedly all day, so its sustainment depended on shorter shared shifts.
`员工需要持续努力才能维持接待台，但任何单个支撑者都无法全天持续工作，因此服务的维持取决于轮流承担较短班次。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sustain13 -->
The staff needed sustained effort to sustain the desk, and no single sustainer could work sustainedly all day, so its sustainment depended on shorter shared shifts.
`员工需要持续努力才能维持接待台，但任何单个支撑者都无法全天持续工作，因此服务的维持取决于轮流承担较短班次。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sustain14 -->
The staff needed sustained effort to sustain the desk, and no single sustainer could work sustainedly all day, so its sustainment depended on shorter shared shifts.
`员工需要持续努力才能维持接待台，但任何单个支撑者都无法全天持续工作，因此服务的维持取决于轮流承担较短班次。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sustain15 -->
Try to buy wood that you know has come from a sustainable source.
`尽量购买你知道来自可持续来源的木材。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sustain16 -->
The creation of an efficient and sustainable transport system is critical to the long-term future of London.
`建设高效、可持续的交通系统对伦敦的长远未来至关重要。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sustain17 -->
For a future walk in a nature reserve, the pupils wanted a sustainable source of timber for signs and a plan to use it sustainably, making ecological sustainability a question about resources rather than a green colour on the poster.
`为了未来去自然保护区的步行活动，学生希望指示牌木材来自可持续来源，也希望以可持续方式使用，让生态可持续性成为资源问题，而不是海报上的绿色。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sustain18 -->
…the growing concern about environmental sustainability.
`……对环境可持续性日益增加的关注。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sustain18 -->
…doubts about the sustainability of the current economic expansion.
`……对当前经济扩张能否持续的疑虑。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sustain19 -->
The state provided a basic quantity of food for daily sustenance, but little else.
`国家提供基本数量的食物供日常维生，此外几乎没有别的东西。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sustain20 -->
The soup would sustain the helpers until evening and provide daily sustenance, but its maker earned her sustenance from paid catering, and a sufficiency of food depended on counting guests rather than guessing.
`汤能为帮手们维持体力到傍晚，提供日常滋养，但制作者靠有偿餐饮谋生；要有足量食物，就得清点来客，而不是猜测。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sustain21 -->
It seemed incredible that he had trusted so incredible a promise, and once its unsupported reach was clear, the claim was unsustainable.
`他竟相信了如此难以采信的承诺，这本身令人惊讶；一旦看清其缺乏依据的外推，这项主张便无法成立。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:sustain22 -->
An afternoon plan could be sustainable even when a permanent version was unsustainable, and testing its sustainability meant checking whether the centre would otherwise spend unsustainably beyond its income.
`一份下午使用的方案可能可以维持，而永久采用却无法持续；检验其持续能力，就意味着检查中心是否会以难以为继的程度支出超过收入。`

<!-- WORD:W045 -->
# transparent

<!-- EXAMPLE:transparent01 -->
…a sheet of transparent coloured plastic.
`……一张透明的彩色塑料薄片。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:transparent02 -->
The company has to make its accounts and operations as transparent as possible.
`公司必须使账目和运营尽可能公开透明。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:transparent03 -->
He thought he could fool people with transparent deceptions.
`他以为能用一眼就能识破的骗术愚弄别人。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:transparent04 -->
A claim of universal savings would have been a transparent deception, since their limited knowledge was transparent and presenting it as certainty would be transparently wrong.
`宣称人人都能省钱会是一个轻易便能识破的骗术，因为他们知识有限这一点显而易见，把它说成确定结论显然错误。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:transparent05 -->
In the science corner, a specially labelled window was transparent to the stated infrared range, so the demonstration concerned transmission of that radiation rather than ordinary visibility alone.
`科学角中，一块专门标注的窗口可让指定波段的红外线透过，因此演示关注的是该辐射的透射，而不仅是普通可见度。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:transparent06 -->
The system was clearly not functioning smoothly or transparently.
`这个系统显然运作得既不顺畅也不透明。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:transparent07 -->
To force this agreement on the nation is transparently wrong.
`强迫全国接受这项协议显然是错误的。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:transparent08 -->
Behind a transparent cover, a transparency of the old room was projected onto a screen, and the transparentness of the cover, unlike the clouded film's lost transparency, allowed every mark to be seen.
`透明罩后，一张旧房间的幻灯正片被投到屏幕上；罩子的透明性不同于浑浊胶片已经减弱的透明度，让每个标记都能清楚看见。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:transparent09 -->
Behind a transparent cover, a transparency of the old room was projected onto a screen, and the transparentness of the cover, unlike the clouded film's lost transparency, allowed every mark to be seen.
`透明罩后，一张旧房间的幻灯正片被投到屏幕上；罩子的透明性不同于浑浊胶片已经减弱的透明度，让每个标记都能清楚看见。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:transparent10 -->
Cataracts is a condition that affects the transparency of the lenses.
`白内障是一种影响晶状体透明度的疾病。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:transparent11 -->
The Chancellor emphasised his determination to promote openness and transparency in the Government's economic decision-making.
`财政大臣强调，他决心促进政府经济决策的开放与透明。`

<!-- WORD:W046 -->
# uncertain

<!-- EXAMPLE:uncertain01 -->
He was uncertain about his brother's intentions.
`他不清楚哥哥的意图。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:uncertain01 -->
They were uncertain of the total value of the transaction.
`他们不确定交易的总价值。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:uncertain01 -->
He stopped, uncertain how to put the question tactfully.
`他停下来，不知道怎样才能委婉地提出这个问题。`
